	do

		local lastBackspaceTime = 0.05

		local function fn14()
			local now = os.clock()
			if now < n10 then
				return autoKickMinGeneration
			end
			n10 = now + 0.5

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return localPlayer:GetNetworkPing()
			end)

			if ok and type(result) == "number" then
				autoKickMinGeneration = math.clamp(result, 0, 0.35)
			end

		end

		local function fn15(arg, arg2)
			local n11 = n5 * n5
			local n12 = arg2:Dot(arg2) - n11
			local n13 = 2 * arg:Dot(arg2)
			local v13 = arg:Dot(arg)
			local n14

			if math.abs(n12) < 0.0001 then
				n14 = nil

				if math.abs(n13) > 0.0001 then
					n14 = -v13 / n13
					local v14 = nil

					if not (n14 > 0) then
						n14 = v14
					end
				end
			else
				local n15 = n13 * n13 - 4 * n12 * v13
				n14 = nil

				if n15 >= 0 then
					local v14 = math.sqrt(n15)
					local n16 = (-n13 - v14) / 2 * n12
					local n17 = (-n13 + v14) / 2 * n12

					if n16 > 0 and n17 > 0 then
						n14 = math.min(n16, n17)
					elseif n16 > 0 then
						n14 = n16
					else
						n14 = nil

						if n17 > 0 then
							n14 = n17
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return math.clamp(n14 or arg.Magnitude / n5, 0, 3)
		end

		local function fn16(arg, arg2)
			local v13 = fn13(arg)
			if not v13 then
				return arg2.Position
			end

			local n11 = arg2.Position + assemblyLinearVelocity * fn14()
			return n11 + assemblyLinearVelocity * fn15(n11 - v13, assemblyLinearVelocity)
		end

		local function fn17(arg)
			local v13 = fn10()
			if not autoStealEnabled and not stealDropEnabled and not stealFilterEnabled or not v13 then

			if not v14 then

		local function fn23(arg, arg2)
			if arg == str2 and arg2 == "rebirth" then
				Library.Notify("Tool unavailable", "Grapple Hook requires 3 Rebirths.", 5)
				return

		end

		local function fn24(arg)
			local character_ = localPlayer.Character
			local humanoid = character_ and character_:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not character_ or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not fn8(arg) then
				return nil
			end
			n23 = os.clock() + 1.5
			if arg.Parent == character_ then
				return arg
			end

			if not (character_:FindFirstChild("RightHand") or character_:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")) then
				if not character_:WaitForChild("RightHand", 1.5) then
					character_:WaitForChild("Right Arm", 1.5)
				end
			end

			pcall(function()
				humanoid:EquipTool(arg)
			end)

			local now3 = os.clock()

			while os.clock() - now3 < 0.6 do
				if arg.Parent ~= character_ then
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
					continue
				end
				break
			end

			if arg.Parent ~= character_ and humanoid.Health > 0 then
				pcall(function()
					humanoid:EquipTool(arg)
				end)

				local now4 = os.clock()

				while os.clock() - now4 < 0.6 do
					if arg.Parent ~= character_ then
						RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
						continue
					end
					break
				end
			end

			if arg.Parent ~= character_ then
				pcall(function()
					arg.Parent = character_
				end)
			end

			if arg.Parent == character_ then
				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				return arg
			end
			local v27 = character_:FindFirstChild(v21)
			return fn8(v27) and v27 or nil
		end

		local function fn25()
			local grappleHook = fn4(v21)

				if not fn8(grappleHook) then
					fn23("Grapple Hook", v27)
					return false
				end
			elseif not fn8(grappleHook) then
				Library.Notify("Tool unavailable", v21 .. " is not currently owned.", 5)
				return false
			end

			v16 = fn24(grappleHook)
			if not v16 then
				Library.Notify("Tool unavailable", "Unable to equip " .. v21 .. " right now.", 5)
				return false

			stealPriorityEnabled = true
			autoKickEnabled = true

		end

		local function fn33(arg, arg2)
			local parent = arg.Parent

			if v25 ~= parent then
				v25 = parent
				raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { parent }
			end

			local n26

			if arg2.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
				n26 = 3
			else
				n26 = math.max(arg.Size.Y * 0.5, arg2.HipHeight + arg.Size.Y * 0.5)
			end

			local hit = Workspace:Raycast(arg.Position, Vector3.new(0, -(n26 + n7), 0), raycastParams)
			return hit ~= nil and hit.Normal.Y > 0.15
		end

		local connection9 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
			local v27, v28 = fn14()

						n21 = -1
					elseif n20 <= n26 then
						n20 = n26
						n21 = 1
					end
				end

			else

				v30 = nil

				if flag20 then

				end
			end

				end
			end
		end)

		local function fn16(arg)
			if not arg or tbl2.ProtectedHumanoid ~= arg or arg.Health <= 0 then
				return false
			end

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return arg.BreakJointsOnDeath == false and arg.RequiresNeck == false and arg:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) == false
			end)

			return ok and result == true
		end

		local function fn17(arg, arg2)
			for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("Weld") or descendant:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
					local part0 = descendant.Part0
					local part1 = descendant.Part1

					if not (part0 and not part0:IsDescendantOf(arg)) then
						local autoKickEnabled = part1 and not part1:IsDescendantOf(arg)
						local v17 = nil
						local v18 = nil

						if autoKickEnabled then
							local v19 = part0
							part0 = part1
							part1 = v19
						else
							part0 = v17
							part1 = v18
						end
					end

					if part0 and part0:IsA("BasePart") and part1 and part1:IsA("BasePart") and part1:IsDescendantOf(arg) and (part0.Position - arg2.Position).Magnitude <= 5 then
						return part0, part1
					end
				end
			end

			return nil
		end

		local function fn18(arg)
			if localPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
				return nil
			end
			local character_ = localPlayer.Character
			local tbl5 = { arg }

			if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Parent and humanoidRootPart ~= arg then
				tbl5[#tbl5 + 1] = humanoidRootPart
			end

			local v17, v18, v19 = ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren())
			local huge = math.huge
			local v20 = nil
						for _, v21 in v17, v18, v19 do
				if v21:IsA("Model") and v21 ~= character_ and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(v21) and v21.Name:sub(-6) ~= "_Clone" then
					local rootPart = v21:FindFirstChild("RootPart") or v21.PrimaryPart or v21:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

					if rootPart and rootPart:IsA("BasePart") then
						for i_, v22 in ipairs(tbl5) do
							local v23, v24 = fn17(v21, v22)

							if v23 and v24 then
								local v25 = v22.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(v23.Position)
								local magnitude = (v24.Position - v22.Position).Magnitude

								if math.abs(v25.X) <= 5 and v25.Y >= -3 and v25.Y <= 7 and v25.Z >= -7 and v25.Z <= 4 and magnitude <= 14 then
									local n14 = (v23.Position - v22.Position).Magnitude + magnitude * 0.05 + (i_ - 1) * 0.01

									if n14 < huge then
										huge = n14
										v20 = v21
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return v20
		end

		local function fn19()
			if localPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true and v15 and v15.Parent then

			return nil
		end

		local function loadChilliLibrary()
			n11 += 1
			local v17 = n11
			v15 = nil
			v16 = nil
			if not flag or not stealDropEnabled or not floatEnabled or localPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
				return

							v15 = v18
							return

	local function fn18(arg)
		if type(arg) ~= "string" then
			return nil
		end
		local match, str = arg:match("%$%s*([%d%.]+)%s*(%a*)")
		local num = tonumber(match)
		if not num then
			return nil
		end

		if str ~= "" then
			local str2 = str:sub(1, 1):upper()
			str = str2 .. str:sub(2):lower()
		end

		return num * (tbl7[str] or 1)
	end

	fn17 = function(arg)
		local v18 = fn18(arg)
		if not v18 then
			return tbl8[#tbl8]
		end

		for _, v19 in ipairs(tbl8) do
			if v18 < v19.Limit then
				return v19
			end
		end

		return tbl8[#tbl8]
	end

		end

		local function fn22(arg)
			local str = tostring(arg or "")
			if str == "" then
				return ""
			end

			if str:match("^%d+$") then
				return "rbxassetid://" .. str
			end
			return str
		end

		local function fn23(arg)
			if not arg or not arg:IsA("Tool") then
				return ""
			end
			local v19 = fn22(arg.TextureId)
			if v19 ~= "" then
				return v19
			end

			for _, v20 in ipairs({ "Icon", "Image", "Thumbnail", "TextureId" }) do
				local attribute = arg:GetAttribute(v20)
				if type(attribute) ~= "string" then
					continue
				end
				v19 = fn22(attribute)
				if v19 ~= "" then
					return v19
				end
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("Decal") or descendant:IsA("Texture") then
					v19 = fn22(descendant.Texture)
				else
					if descendant:IsA("ImageLabel") or descendant:IsA("ImageButton") then
						v19 = fn22(descendant.Image)
					end
				end

				if v19 ~= "" then
					return v19
				end
			end

			return ""
		end

		end

				end

		end

		local function fn26(arg, arg2)

				return Vector3.new(0, 3.1, 0)
			end

			end

		end

		local function fn27(arg, arg2)
			for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("Weld") or descendant:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
					local part0 = descendant.Part0
					local part1 = descendant.Part1
					local v19

					if part0 and not part0:IsDescendantOf(arg) then
						v19 = part0
					else
						local flag9 = part1 and not part1:IsDescendantOf(arg)
						local v20 = nil
						local v21 = nil

						if flag9 then
							v19 = part1
							part1 = part0
						else
							v19 = v20
							part1 = v21
						end
					end

					if v19 and v19:IsA("BasePart") and v19.Parent == Workspace and part1 and part1:IsA("BasePart") and part1:IsDescendantOf(arg) and (v19.Position - arg2.Position).Magnitude <= n8 then
						return v19, part1
					end
				end
			end

			return nil
		end

		local function fn28(arg)
			if type(arg) ~= "string" or not arg:find("/s", 1, true) then
				return false
			end
			local match = arg:match("%$([%d%.]+)")
			local flag9 = match ~= nil

			if flag9 then
				flag9 = (tonumber(match) or 0) > 0
			end

			return flag9
		end

		local function fn29(arg, arg2)
			for k, v19 in pairs(arg:GetAttributes()) do
				if k ~= "__AssetDescendantCount" and arg2:GetAttribute(k) ~= v19 then
					return false
				end
			end

			return true
		end

		local function fn30(arg)
			local generation = arg:FindFirstChild("Generation", true)
			if generation and generation:IsA("TextLabel") and fn28(generation.Text) then
				return generation
			end
			local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
			local debris = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
			if not plots or not debris then
				return nil
			end
			local characterConnections = {}
			local tbl15 = {}
			local v19, v20, v21 = ipairs(plots:GetChildren())
						for _, v22 in v19, v20, v21 do
				for _, child in ipairs(v22:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Model") and child.Name == arg.Name then
						local position = child:GetPivot().Position
						tbl15[#tbl15 + 1] = position

						if fn29(arg, child) then
							characterConnections[#characterConnections + 1] = position
						end
					end
				end
			end

			characterConnections = #characterConnections > 0 and characterConnections or tbl15
			if #characterConnections == 0 then
				return nil
			end
			local mutation = arg:FindFirstChild("Mutation", true)
			local text = mutation and mutation:IsA("TextLabel") and mutation.Text or nil
			local v22, v23, v24 = ipairs(debris:GetChildren())
			local huge = math.huge
			local v25 = nil
			_vm_t_1 = (function(iterator, iteratorState, control) return function() local values = table.pack(iterator(iteratorState, control)); control = values[1]; if control == nil then return false end; return true, table.unpack(values, 1, values.n) end end)(v22, v23, v24)

			for _, v26 in v22, v23, v24 do
				if v26.Name == "FastOverheadTemplate" and v26:IsA("BasePart") then
					local animalOverhead = v26:FindFirstChild("AnimalOverhead")
					local displayName = animalOverhead and animalOverhead:FindFirstChild("DisplayName")
					local generation2 = animalOverhead and animalOverhead:FindFirstChild("Generation")
					animalOverhead = animalOverhead and animalOverhead:FindFirstChild("Mutation")

					if displayName and displayName:IsA("TextLabel") and displayName.Text == arg.Name and generation2 and generation2:IsA("TextLabel") and fn28(generation2.Text) then
						local huge2 = math.huge

						for _, v27 in ipairs(characterConnections) do
							huge2 = math.min(huge2, (v26.Position - v27).Magnitude)
						end

						local flag9 = text and animalOverhead and animalOverhead:IsA("TextLabel") and animalOverhead.Text ~= text
						local n14 = 0

						if flag9 then
							n14 = 1000
						end

						local n15 = huge2 + n14

						if n15 < huge then
							huge = n15
							v25 = generation2
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return v25
		end

		local function fn32(arg)
			if not (arg and arg.Parent and arg.Visible) then
				return nil
			end
			local str = tostring(arg.Text or "")
			if str == "" then
				return nil
			end

			local characterConnections = {
				Text = str,
				RichText = arg.RichText == true,
				Color = arg.TextColor3,
				Font = arg.FontFace,
				Gradient = nil,
				StrokeColor = nil,
			}

			local uiStroke = arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

			if uiStroke then

			end

			local uiGradient = arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")

			if uiGradient then
				characterConnections.Gradient = { Color = uiGradient.Color, Rotation = uiGradient.Rotation }
			end

			return characterConnections
		end

		local function fn33(arg)
			if arg:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
				return nil
			end
			local character_ = arg.Character
			local humanoidRootPart2 = character_ and character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not humanoidRootPart2 then
				return nil
			end
			local huge = math.huge
			local v19 = nil

			for _, child in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Model") and child ~= character_ and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(child) and child.Name:sub(-6) ~= "_Clone" then
					local rootPart = child:FindFirstChild("RootPart") or child.PrimaryPart or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

					if rootPart and rootPart:IsA("BasePart") then
						local v20, v21 = fn27(child, humanoidRootPart2)

						if v20 and v21 then
							local v22 = humanoidRootPart2.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(v20.Position)
							local magnitude = (v21.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude

							if math.abs(v22.X) <= 4 and v22.Y >= -2 and v22.Y <= 6 and v22.Z >= -6 and v22.Z <= 3 and magnitude <= autoKickMinGeneration then
								local n14 = (v20.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude + magnitude * 0.05

								if n14 < huge then
									huge = n14
									v19 = child
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if not v19 then
				return nil
			end
			local v20 = fn30(v19)
			return v19.Name, v19, v20 and v20.Text or nil, v20, fn31(v19)
		end

		local function fn34()
			local currentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

		end

		local function fn35(text, font3, size)
			local getTextBoundsParams = Instance.new("GetTextBoundsParams")
			getTextBoundsParams.Text = text
			getTextBoundsParams.Font = font3
			getTextBoundsParams.Size = size
			getTextBoundsParams.Width = 1000

			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return TextService:GetTextBoundsAsync(getTextBoundsParams)
			end)

			getTextBoundsParams:Destroy()
			if ok then
				return result.X
			end
			return (utf8.len(text) or #text) * size * 0.56
		end

		end

			local MutationText = createTextLabel("MutationText", frame, 4)
			MutationText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			MutationText.RichText = true
			local uiStroke3 = Instance.new("UIStroke")
			fn36(uiStroke3, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), 0.05, 1.5)
			uiStroke3.Transparency = 0.15
			uiStroke3.Parent = MutationText
			local uiGradient5 = Instance.new("UIGradient")
			uiGradient5.Enabled = false
			uiGradient5.Rotation = 90
			uiGradient5.Parent = MutationText
			local GenerationShadow = createTextLabel("GenerationShadow", frame, 3)
			GenerationShadow.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 48, 0)
			GenerationShadow.TextTransparency = 0.08
			local GenerationText = createTextLabel("GenerationText", frame, 4)
			GenerationText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(115, 255, 0)
			local uiStroke4 = Instance.new("UIStroke")
			fn36(uiStroke4, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), 0.05, 1.5)
			uiStroke4.Transparency = 0.3
			uiStroke4.Parent = GenerationText
			local imageLabel2 = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
			imageLabel2.Name = "ToolShadow"
			local vector22 = Vector2.new
			imageLabel2.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			imageLabel2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			imageLabel2.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
			imageLabel2.ImageTransparency = 0.35
			imageLabel2.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
			imageLabel2.Visible = false
			imageLabel2.ZIndex = 1
			imageLabel2.Parent = frame
			local uiAspectRatioConstraint2 = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
			uiAspectRatioConstraint2.AspectRatio = 1
			uiAspectRatioConstraint2.Parent = imageLabel2
			local imageLabel3 = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
			imageLabel3.Name = "ToolIcon"
			local vector23 = Vector2.new
			imageLabel3.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			imageLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			imageLabel3.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
			imageLabel3.Visible = false
			imageLabel3.ZIndex = 2
			imageLabel3.Parent = frame
			local uiAspectRatioConstraint3 = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
			uiAspectRatioConstraint3.AspectRatio = 1
			uiAspectRatioConstraint3.Parent = imageLabel3
			local frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
			frame2.Name = "AdminLine"
			local vector24 = Vector2.new
			frame2.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			frame2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
			frame2.Visible = false
			frame2.ZIndex = 3
			frame2.Parent = frame
			local AdminShadow = createTextLabel("AdminShadow", frame2, 3)
			local vector25 = Vector2.new
			AdminShadow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			AdminShadow.FontFace = font
			AdminShadow.Text = "Admin Panel"
			AdminShadow.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 14, 22)
			AdminShadow.TextTransparency = 0.08
			local AdminText = createTextLabel("AdminText", frame2, 4)
			local vector26 = Vector2.new
			AdminText.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			AdminText.FontFace = font
			AdminText.Text = "Admin Panel"
			AdminText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			local uiStroke5 = Instance.new("UIStroke")
			fn36(uiStroke5, Color3.fromRGB(10, 24, 45), 0.02, 1.45)
			uiStroke5.Transparency = 0.02
			uiStroke5.Parent = AdminText
			local uiGradient6 = Instance.new("UIGradient")
			local colorSequence = ColorSequence.new
			local characterConnections = {}
			local v20 = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
			local v21 = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, Color3.fromRGB(235, 242, 255))
			characterConnections[1] = v20
			characterConnections[2] = v21

			do
				local values = table.pack(ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)))
				table.move(values, 1, values.n, 3, characterConnections)
			end

			uiGradient6.Color = colorSequence(characterConnections)
			uiGradient6.Rotation = 90
			uiGradient6.Parent = AdminText

			local tbl15 = {
				TargetPlayer = arg,
				PlayerHighlight = highlight,
				NetworkTracker = boxHandleAdornment,
				NetworkAnchor = v19,
				VisualHead = arg2,
				Billboard = billboardGui,
				Avatar = imageLabel,
				NameShadow = NameShadow,
				DisplayName = DisplayName,
				BrainrotShadow = BrainrotShadow,
				BrainrotText = BrainrotText,
				BrainrotGradient = uiGradient4,
				BrainrotStrokeGradient = uiGradient3,
				GenerationShadow = GenerationShadow,
				GenerationText = GenerationText,
				MutationShadow = MutationShadow,
				MutationText = MutationText,
				MutationGradient = uiGradient5,
				ToolShadow = imageLabel2,
				ToolIcon = imageLabel3,
				AdminLine = frame2,
				AdminShadow = AdminShadow,
				AdminText = AdminText,
				BrainrotName = nil,
				BrainrotGeneration = nil,
				BrainrotMutation = nil,
				IsAdmin = false,
				HasTool = false,
			}

			fn37(tbl15, false)
			return tbl15
		end

		local function fn39(arg)
			local tag = arg.Tag
			local character_ = arg.Character
			if not tag or not character_ then
				return false
			end
			local visualHead = tag.VisualHead
			local networkAnchor = tag.NetworkAnchor
			if not visualHead or not visualHead:IsDescendantOf(character_) or not networkAnchor or not networkAnchor:IsDescendantOf(character_) then
				return false
			end
			tag.Billboard.Adornee = networkAnchor
			local visible = networkAnchor ~= visualHead and (networkAnchor.Position - visualHead.Position).Magnitude >= n10
			tag.PlayerHighlight.Enabled = not visible
			tag.NetworkTracker.Adornee = networkAnchor
			tag.NetworkTracker.Visible = visible
			return true
		end

		local function fn40(arg)
			if arg:GetAttribute("AdminCommands") == true then
				return true
			end
			local attribute = arg:GetAttribute("Role")
			return type(attribute) == "string" and string.find(string.lower(attribute), "admin", 1, true) ~= nil
		end

		local function fn41(arg, arg2)
			if not arg.Tag then
				return
			end
			arg.Tag.IsAdmin = fn40(arg2)
			fn37(arg.Tag, arg.Tag.HasTool == true)
		end

		local function fn42(arg)
			if not arg.Tag or not arg.Character then
				return
			end
			local v19 = fn23(fn24(arg.Character))
			local visible = v19 ~= ""
			arg.Tag.ToolIcon.Image = v19
			arg.Tag.ToolIcon.Visible = visible
			arg.Tag.ToolShadow.Image = v19
			arg.Tag.ToolShadow.Visible = visible
			fn37(arg.Tag, visible)
		end

		local function fn43(arg)
			if not arg.Tag then
				return
			end
			arg.Tag.BrainrotName = arg.BrainrotName
			arg.Tag.BrainrotGeneration = arg.BrainrotGeneration
			arg.Tag.BrainrotMutation = fn32(arg.BrainrotMutationLabel)
			fn37(arg.Tag, arg.Tag.HasTool == true)
			local v19 = fn17(arg.BrainrotGeneration)

			if arg.Tag.BrainrotGradient then
				arg.Tag.BrainrotGradient.Color = v19.Text
			end

			if arg.Tag.BrainrotStrokeGradient then
				arg.Tag.BrainrotStrokeGradient.Color = v19.Stroke
			end

			if arg.BrainrotHighlight and (tbl10.Brainrot ~= true or arg.BrainrotHighlight.Adornee ~= arg.BrainrotModel) then
				arg.BrainrotHighlight:Destroy()
				arg.BrainrotHighlight = nil
			end

			if tbl10.Brainrot == true and arg.BrainrotModel and arg.BrainrotModel.Parent and not arg.BrainrotHighlight then
				local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
				highlight.Name = "BrainrotHighlight"
				highlight.Adornee = arg.BrainrotModel
				highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
				highlight.FillTransparency = 0.68
				highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
				highlight.Parent = v18
				arg.BrainrotHighlight = highlight
			end

			if arg.BrainrotHighlight then
				arg.BrainrotHighlight.FillColor = v19.Fill
				arg.BrainrotHighlight.OutlineColor = v19.Outline
			end
		end

		local function fn44(arg)
			if arg.BrainrotGenerationConnection then
				arg.BrainrotGenerationConnection:Disconnect()
				arg.BrainrotGenerationConnection = nil
			end

			local v19 = ipairs
			local brainrotMutationConnections = arg.BrainrotMutationConnections or {}

			for _, brainrotMutationConnection in v19(brainrotMutationConnections) do
				brainrotMutationConnection:Disconnect()
			end

			arg.BrainrotMutationConnections = nil
			arg.BrainrotMutationLabel = nil
		end

		local function fn45(arg, brainrotMutationLabel, arg2)
			if not brainrotMutationLabel then
				return
			end
			arg.BrainrotMutationLabel = brainrotMutationLabel
			local brainrotMutationConnections = {}

			local function fn46()
				if arg.BrainrotModel == arg2 then
					fn43(arg)
				end
			end

			brainrotMutationConnections[#brainrotMutationConnections + 1] = brainrotMutationLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(fn46)
			brainrotMutationConnections[#brainrotMutationConnections + 1] = brainrotMutationLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(fn46)
			brainrotMutationConnections[#brainrotMutationConnections + 1] = brainrotMutationLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextColor3"):Connect(fn46)
			arg.BrainrotMutationConnections = brainrotMutationConnections
		end

		local function fn46(arg, arg2)
			arg.StealingVersion = arg.StealingVersion + 1
			local stealingVersion = arg.StealingVersion

			if arg2:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
				fn44(arg)
				arg.BrainrotName = nil
				arg.BrainrotGeneration = nil
				arg.BrainrotModel = nil
				fn43(arg)
				return
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				local _vm_M_1 = 1

				for i_ = 1, 7 do
					if not autoKickEnabled or arg.StealingVersion ~= stealingVersion or tbl11[arg2] ~= arg then
						return
					end
					local v19, v20, v21, v22, v23 = fn33(arg2)

					if v19 then
						fn44(arg)
						arg.BrainrotName = v19
						arg.BrainrotModel = v20
						arg.BrainrotGeneration = v21
						fn45(arg, v23, v20)

						if v22 then
							local function fn47()
								if arg.BrainrotModel == v20 then
									arg.BrainrotGeneration = v22.Text
									fn43(arg)
								end
							end

							arg.BrainrotGenerationConnection = v22:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(fn47)
						end

						fn43(arg)
						return
					end

					if i_ < 7 then
						task.wait(0.12)
					end
				end

				if arg.StealingVersion == stealingVersion then
					fn44(arg)
					arg.BrainrotName = nil
					arg.BrainrotGeneration = nil
					arg.BrainrotModel = nil
					fn43(arg)
				end
			end)
		end

		local function fn47(arg, arg2, arg3)
			local image = playerConnections[arg2.UserId]

			if image == nil then
				local ok

				ok, image = pcall(function()
					return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(arg2.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
				end)

				image = ok and image or ""
				playerConnections[arg2.UserId] = image
			end

			if autoKickEnabled and arg.Version == arg3 and arg.Tag and arg.Tag.Avatar.Parent then
				arg.Tag.Avatar.Image = image
			end
		end

		local flag9 = false

		local function fn48(arg)
			if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
				return arg.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart"
			end
			return arg:IsA("Decal") or arg:IsA("Texture")
		end

		local function fn49(arg, arg2)
			if flag9 or not arg2.Parent then
				return
			end
			local transparency2 = arg2.Transparency

			if transparency2 <= n11 then
				arg.RevealOriginals[arg2] = transparency2
				arg.RevealHidden[arg2] = nil
				return
			end

			if arg.RevealOriginals[arg2] == nil then
				return
			end
			arg.RevealHidden[arg2] = transparency2
			flag9 = true

			pcall(function()
				arg2.Transparency = transparency

				if arg2:IsA("BasePart") then
					arg2.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
				end
			end)

			flag9 = false
		end

		local function fn50(arg, arg2)
			if arg.RevealBound[arg2] then
				return
			end
			arg.RevealBound[arg2] = true
			local transparency2 = arg2.Transparency

			if transparency2 <= n11 then
				arg.RevealOriginals[arg2] = transparency2
			else
				if arg2:IsA("BasePart") and tbl6[arg2.Name] then
					arg.RevealOriginals[arg2] = 0
				elseif arg2:IsA("Decal") and string.lower(arg2.Name) == "face" then
					arg.RevealOriginals[arg2] = 0
				end
			end

			arg.RevealConnections[#arg.RevealConnections + 1] = arg2:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
				if autoKickEnabled then
					fn49(arg, arg2)
				end
			end)

			fn49(arg, arg2)
		end

		local function fn51(arg, arg2)
			for _, descendant in ipairs(arg2:GetDescendants()) do
				if fn48(descendant) then
					fn50(arg, descendant)
				end
			end

			arg.RevealConnections[#arg.RevealConnections + 1] = arg2.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
				if autoKickEnabled and fn48(descendant) then
					fn50(arg, descendant)
				end
			end)
		end

		local function fn52(arg)
			fn19(arg.RevealConnections)
			flag9 = true

			for k, v19 in pairs(arg.RevealHidden) do
				if k.Parent then
					pcall(function()
						k.Transparency = v19
					end)
				end
			end

			flag9 = false
			table.clear(arg.RevealHidden)
			table.clear(arg.RevealOriginals)
			table.clear(arg.RevealBound)
		end

		local function fn53(arg)
			fn19(arg.CharacterConnections)
			fn20(arg)
			fn52(arg)
			fn44(arg)

			if arg.BrainrotHighlight then
				arg.BrainrotHighlight:Destroy()
				arg.BrainrotHighlight = nil
			end

			arg.BrainrotModel = nil
			arg.BrainrotGeneration = nil

			if arg.Tag then
				if arg.Tag.PlayerHighlight then
					arg.Tag.PlayerHighlight:Destroy()
				end

				if arg.Tag.NetworkTracker then
					arg.Tag.NetworkTracker:Destroy()
				end

				arg.Tag.Billboard:Destroy()
				arg.Tag = nil
			end

			arg.Character = nil
			arg.BindingCharacter = nil
		end

		local fn54 = nil

		fn54 = function(arg, arg2, character_)
			fn53(arg)
			arg.Version = arg.Version + 1
			local version = arg.Version
			if not autoKickEnabled or not character_ then
				return
			end
			arg.Character = character_
			arg.BindingCharacter = character_
			fn21(arg, character_)
			fn51(arg, character_)

			local function fn55()
				task.defer(function()
					if autoKickEnabled and arg.Version == version then
						fn42(arg)
					end
				end)
			end

			local characterConnections = arg.CharacterConnections

			characterConnections[#arg.CharacterConnections + 1] = character_.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if child:IsA("Tool") then
					fn55()
				elseif child:IsA("Humanoid") then
					fn21(arg, character_)
				end
			end)

			local characterConnections2 = arg.CharacterConnections

			characterConnections2[#arg.CharacterConnections + 1] = character_.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
				if child:IsA("Tool") then
					fn55()
				end
			end)

			local characterConnections3 = arg.CharacterConnections
			local n14 = #arg.CharacterConnections + 1

			local function fn56()
				if not autoKickEnabled or arg.Version ~= version or character_ ~= arg2.Character then
					return
				end

				if character_:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
					if not arg.Tag then
						task.defer(function()
							if autoKickEnabled and arg.Version == version then
								fn54(arg, arg2, character_)
							end
						end)
					end

					return
				end

				arg.Version = arg.Version + 1
				fn53(arg)
			end

			characterConnections3[n14] = character_.AncestryChanged:Connect(fn56)

			local function fn57()
				local head = character_:FindFirstChild("Head") or character_:WaitForChild("Head", 5)

				if not autoKickEnabled or arg.Version ~= version or character_ ~= arg2.Character or not character_:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) or not head or not head:IsDescendantOf(character_) then
					if arg.Version == version then
						arg.BindingCharacter = nil
					end

					return
				end

				local v19 = fn25(character_, head)
				arg.Tag = fn38(arg2, head, v19)
				fn39(arg)
				arg.BindingCharacter = nil

				arg.CharacterConnections[#arg.CharacterConnections + 1] = head.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
					if not autoKickEnabled or arg.Version ~= version or head:IsDescendantOf(character_) then
						return
					end
					arg.Version = arg.Version + 1
					fn53(arg)
					local character_2 = arg2.Character

					if character_2 and character_2:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
						task.defer(function()
							if autoKickEnabled and tbl11[arg2] == arg then
								fn54(arg, arg2, character_2)
							end
						end)
					end
				end)

				fn41(arg, arg2)
				fn42(arg)
				fn46(arg, arg2)
				fn47(arg, arg2, version)
			end

			task.spawn(fn57)
		end

		local function fn55(player)
			local v19 = tbl11[player]
			if not v19 then
				return
			end
			v19.Version = v19.Version + 1
			v19.StealingVersion = v19.StealingVersion + 1
			fn53(v19)
			fn19(v19.PlayerConnections)
			tbl11[player] = nil
		end

		local function fn56(player)
			if player == localPlayer or tbl11[player] then
				return
			end

			local characterConnections = {
				Version = 0,
				StealingVersion = 0,
				Character = nil,
				BindingCharacter = nil,
				Tag = nil,
				BrainrotName = nil,
				BrainrotGeneration = nil,
				BrainrotGenerationConnection = nil,
				BrainrotModel = nil,
				BrainrotHighlight = nil,
				NameDisplayHumanoid = nil,
				NameDisplayDistance = nil,
				RevealOriginals = {},
				RevealHidden = {},
				RevealBound = {},
				RevealConnections = {},
				CharacterConnections = {},
				PlayerConnections = {},
			}

			tbl11[player] = characterConnections

			for _, v19 in ipairs({ "AdminCommands", "Role" }) do
				local playerConnections = characterConnections.PlayerConnections
				local n14 = #characterConnections.PlayerConnections + 1

				local function fn57()
					if autoKickEnabled and tbl11[player] == characterConnections then
						fn41(characterConnections, player)
					end
				end

				playerConnections[n14] = player:GetAttributeChangedSignal(v19):Connect(fn57)
			end

			local playerConnections = characterConnections.PlayerConnections
			local n14 = #characterConnections.PlayerConnections + 1

			local function fn57(character_)
				fn54(characterConnections, player, character_)
			end

			playerConnections[n14] = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(fn57)

			characterConnections.PlayerConnections[#characterConnections.PlayerConnections + 1] = player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(character_)
				if characterConnections.Character == character_ then
					characterConnections.Version = characterConnections.Version + 1
					fn53(characterConnections)
				end
			end)

			characterConnections.PlayerConnections[#characterConnections.PlayerConnections + 1] = player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Character"):Connect(function()
				task.defer(function()
					if not autoKickEnabled or tbl11[player] ~= characterConnections then
						return
					end
					local character_ = player.Character

					if character_ ~= characterConnections.Character then
						fn54(characterConnections, player, character_)
					elseif characterConnections.Tag then
						local adornee = characterConnections.Tag.Billboard.Adornee

						if not adornee or not adornee:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
							fn54(characterConnections, player, character_)
						end
					end
				end)
			end)

			local playerConnections2 = characterConnections.PlayerConnections
			local n15 = #characterConnections.PlayerConnections + 1

			local function fn58()
				fn46(characterConnections, player)
			end

			playerConnections2[n15] = player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(fn58)
			fn54(characterConnections, player, player.Character)
		end

		local function fn57()
			for k, v19 in pairs(tbl11) do
				local character_ = k.Character

				if character_ and character_:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
					local tag = v19.Tag
					local billboard = tag and tag.Billboard
					tag = tag and tag.PlayerHighlight
					local adornee = billboard and billboard.Adornee
					local parent = billboard and billboard.Parent and adornee and adornee:IsDescendantOf(character_) and tag and tag.Parent and fn39(v19)

					if (v19.Character ~= character_ or not parent) and v19.BindingCharacter ~= character_ then
						fn54(v19, k, character_)
					end
				elseif v19.Character then
					v19.Version = v19.Version + 1
					fn53(v19)
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn58()
			autoKickEnabled = false
			n13 += 1
			fn19(tbl12)
			local characterConnections = {}

			for k in pairs(tbl11) do
				characterConnections[#characterConnections + 1] = k
			end

			for _, v19 in ipairs(characterConnections) do
				fn55(v19)
			end
		end

		local function fn59()
			for _, v19 in pairs(tbl11) do
				if v19.Tag and v19.Tag.Billboard.Parent then
					fn37(v19.Tag, v19.Tag.HasTool == true)
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn60()
			for _, v19 in pairs(tbl11) do
				if v19.Tag and v19.Tag.Billboard.Parent then
					fn42(v19)
					fn43(v19)
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn61()
			fn58()
			autoKickEnabled = true
			local v19 = n13
			tbl12[#tbl12 + 1] = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(fn56)
			tbl12[#tbl12 + 1] = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(fn55)
			local currentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

			if currentCamera then
				tbl12[#tbl12 + 1] = currentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(fn59)
			end

			for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				fn56(player)
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				while autoKickEnabled and n13 == v19 do
					task.wait(0.2)

					if autoKickEnabled and n13 == v19 then
						fn57()
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		bindableEvent.Event:Connect(fn58)
		screenGui.Destroying:Connect(fn58)

		v13:CreateToggle({
			Name = "Player ESP",
			Default = true,
			Callback = function(arg)
				if arg then
					fn61()
				else
					fn58()
				end
			end,
		})

		v13:CreateMultiDropdown({
			Name = "Show Information",
			Note = "Choose what Player ESP shows. Username replaces the display name.",
			Options = { "Name", "Username", "Avatar", "Tool", "Brainrot", "Admin Panel" },

		local tbl18 = ok3 and type(result3) == "table" and result3 or {}

		for k, v18 in pairs(tbl18) do

				v18.TraitKey = k

					local str = tostring(v18.Icon)
					tbl17[str] = v18
					local match = str:match("%d+")

					if match then
						tbl17[match] = v18
					end
				end
			end
		end

		autoStealEnabled = false
		stealConnections = {}
		tbl5 = {}
		n = 1
		stealDropEnabled = false
		stealFilterEnabled = false

			["K/s"] = { Min = 0, Max = 1000, Mult = 1000 },
			["M/s"] = { Min = 0, Max = 1000, Mult = 1000000 },
			["B/s"] = { Min = 1, Max = 10, Mult = 1e9 },
		}

		local function fn25(arg)
			for _, v18 in ipairs(tbl10) do
				if arg < v18.Limit then
					return v18
				end
			end

		end

		local function fn26(arg)
			local v18 = characterConnections[arg]
			if type(v18) == "table" and v18.Rarity then
				return tostring(v18.Rarity)
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function fn27(arg)
			if arg >= 1e9 then
				return string.format("$%.2fB/s", arg / 1e9)
			end

			if arg >= 1000000 then
				return string.format("$%.2fM/s", arg / 1000000)
			end

			if arg >= 1000 then
				return string.format("$%.1fK/s", arg / 1000)
			end
			return string.format("$%d/s", math.floor(arg))
		end

		local function fn28(arg, arg2)
			local debris = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
			if not debris then
				return nil
			end
			local v18 = nil
			local v19 = nil

			for _, child in ipairs(debris:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == "FastOverheadTemplate" and child:IsA("BasePart") then
					local animalOverhead = child:FindFirstChild("AnimalOverhead")
					local displayName = animalOverhead and animalOverhead:FindFirstChild("DisplayName")

					if displayName and displayName:IsA("TextLabel") and displayName.Text == arg.Name then
						local magnitude = (child.Position - arg2.Position).Magnitude

						if not v18 or magnitude < v18 then
							v18 = magnitude
							v19 = animalOverhead
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if v19 and v18 and v18 <= 14 then
				return v19
			end
			return nil
		end

			if arg.CachedOverhead and arg.CachedOverhead.Parent then
				return arg.CachedOverhead

			arg.CachedOverhead = v18
			return v18
		end

		local function fn30(arg, arg2, arg3)
			local mutation = arg3 or fn28(arg, arg2)
			mutation = mutation and mutation:FindFirstChild("Mutation")
			if not (mutation and mutation:IsA("TextLabel") and mutation.Visible) then
				return nil
			end
			local str = tostring(mutation.Text or "")
			if str == "" then
				return nil
			end

			local tbl19 = {
				Text = str,
				RichText = mutation.RichText == true,
				Color = mutation.TextColor3,
				Font = mutation.FontFace,
				StrokeColor = nil,
				Gradient = nil,
				SourceGrad = mutation:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient"),
			}

			local uiStroke = mutation:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

			if uiStroke then
				tbl19.StrokeColor = uiStroke.Color
			end

			if tbl19.SourceGrad then
				tbl19.Gradient = {
					Color = tbl19.SourceGrad.Color,
					Rotation = tbl19.SourceGrad.Rotation,
					Offset = tbl19.SourceGrad.Offset,
				}
			end

			return tbl19
		end

		local function fn31(arg)
			if not arg or arg == "" then
				return nil
			end
			local match, v18 = arg:gsub("[%$,/s]", ""):gsub("%s+", ""):match("^([%d%.]+)([KkMmBbTt]?)$")

			if match then
				local n6 = tonumber(match) or 0
				local str = v18:upper()

				if str == "K" then
					n6 *= 1000
				elseif str == "M" then
					n6 *= 1000000
				elseif str == "B" then
					n6 *= 1e9
				elseif str == "T" then
					n6 *= 1e12
				end

				return n6
			end

			return nil
		end

		local function fn32(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
			local v18 = characterConnections[arg]
			local n6 = type(v18) == "table" and tonumber(v18.Generation) or 0
			if n6 <= 0 then
				return fn31(arg4) or 0
			end
			local v19 = arg2 and tbl16 and tbl16[arg2]
			local n7 = 1

			if v19 then
				n7 = 1 + (tonumber(tbl16[arg2].Modifier) or 0)
			end

			local v20 = arg3 and tbl18
			local flag11 = false

			if v20 then
				for _, v21 in ipairs(arg3) do
					local v22 = tbl18[v21] or tbl18[v21:gsub("_", " ")]

					if v22 then
						if v21 == "Sleepy" or v22.Name == "Sleepy" then
							flag11 = true
						else
							n7 += tonumber(v22.MultiplierModifier) or 0
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local v21 = math.round(n6 * n7 * (flag11 and 0.5 or 1))
			local v22 = fn31(arg4)
			if v22 and v22 > v21 then
				return v22
			end
			return v21 > 0 and v21 or v22 or n6
		end

		local function fn33(arg, arg2, arg3)
			local tbl19 = {}
			local tbl20 = {}
			local tbl21 = {}
			local tbl22 = {}

			local function fn34(arg4, arg5)
				if arg4 and not tbl22[arg4] then
					tbl22[arg4] = true
					table.insert(tbl20, arg4)
				end

				if arg5 and arg5 ~= "" and arg5 ~= "rbxassetid://110835412437000" then
					local match = tostring(arg5):match("%d+")
					local str = match or tostring(arg5)

					if not tbl21[str] then
						tbl21[str] = true
						local str2 = tostring(arg5)

						if not str2:find("://") and match then
							str2 = "rbxassetid://" .. match
						end

						table.insert(tbl19, str2)
					end
				end
			end

			local traits = arg3 or fn28(arg, arg2)
			traits = traits and traits:FindFirstChild("Traits")

			if traits and traits.Visible then
				for _, child in ipairs(traits:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("ImageLabel") and child.Visible and child.Image and child.Image ~= "" then
						local str = tostring(child.Image)
						local match = str:match("%d+")
						local traitKey = tbl17[str] or match and tbl17[match]

						if traitKey then
							traitKey = traitKey.TraitKey or traitKey.Display or traitKey.Name
						end

						fn34(traitKey, child.Image)
					end
				end
			end

			for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
				local match = child.Name:match("^_Trait%.(.+)$")

				if match then
					local v18 = tbl18[match] or tbl18[match:gsub("_", " ")]
					fn34(match, v18 and v18.Icon)
				end
			end

			local attribute = arg:GetAttribute("Trait") or arg:GetAttribute("Traits")

			if attribute then
				local str = tostring(attribute)
				local v18 = tbl18[str] or tbl18[str:gsub("_", " ")]
				fn34(str, v18 and v18.Icon)
			end

			return #tbl19 > 0 and tbl19 or nil, #tbl20 > 0 and tbl20 or nil
		end

	local function fn33(parent, arg, arg2)
		if not (arg2 and arg2.Parent) then
			return false
		end

		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return arg2:Clone()
		end)

		if not ok or not result then
			return false
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(result:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Beam") or descendant:IsA("Trail") or descendant:IsA("Light") or descendant:IsA("Sound") or descendant:IsA("Script") or descendant:IsA("LocalScript") then
				descendant:Destroy()
			end
		end

		local worldModel = Instance.new("WorldModel")
		worldModel.Parent = parent
		result.Parent = worldModel

		pcall(function()
			result:PivotTo(CFrame.new())
		end)

		local cframe = CFrame.new()
		local vector = Vector3.new

		local ok2, result2, result3 = pcall(function()
			return result:GetBoundingBox()
		end)

		ok2 = ok2 and result2 and result3
		local vector2 = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)

		if not ok2 then
			result3 = vector2
			result2 = cframe
		end

		local n8 = math.max(result3.X, result3.Y, result3.Z)
		local position = result2.Position
		local autoKickMinGeneration = math.max(n8 * 1.35, 3.5)
		arg.FieldOfView = 40
		local cframe2 = CFrame.lookAt
		local n10 = position + Vector3.new(autoKickMinGeneration * 0.28, autoKickMinGeneration * 0.08, -autoKickMinGeneration)
		local vector3 = Vector3.new
		arg.CFrame = cframe2(n10, position, Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
		local humanoid = arg2:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
		humanoid = humanoid or arg2:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AnimationController")
		humanoid = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
		local humanoid2 = result:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") or result:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AnimationController")
		local animator = humanoid2 and humanoid2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

		if humanoid and animator then
			local playingAnimationTracks = humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
			playingAnimationTracks = playingAnimationTracks and playingAnimationTracks[1]

			if playingAnimationTracks and playingAnimationTracks.Animation then
				local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
				animation.AnimationId = playingAnimationTracks.Animation.AnimationId

				local ok3, result4 = pcall(function()
					return animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
				end)

				if ok3 and result4 then
					pcall(function()
						result4.Looped = true
						result4:Play(0)
						result4.TimePosition = playingAnimationTracks.TimePosition
						result4:AdjustSpeed(playingAnimationTracks.Speed)
					end)
				end
			end
		end

		return true
	end

	end

		end)

		task.delay(n8 + 1, fn38)
		return true
	end

	local function fn37(arg)
		local match, v24 = tostring(arg or ""):gsub("[%$,/s]", ""):gsub("%s+", ""):match("^([%d%.]+)([KkMmBbTt]?)$")
		local num = tonumber(match)
		if not num then
			return nil
		end
		return num * (({ K = 1000, M = 1000000, B = 1e9, T = 1e12 })[string.upper(v24)] or 1)
	end

	local function fn38(arg)
		if arg >= 1e12 then
			return string.format("$%.2fT/s", arg / 1e12)
		end

		if arg >= 1e9 then
			return string.format("$%.2fB/s", arg / 1e9)
		end

		if arg >= 1000000 then
			return string.format("$%.2fM/s", arg / 1000000)
		end

		if arg >= 1000 then
			return string.format("$%.1fK/s", arg / 1000)
		end
		return string.format("$%d/s", math.floor(arg))
	end

	local function fn39(arg)
		return arg:FindFirstChild("RootPart") or arg.PrimaryPart or arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
	end

	local function fn40()
		local tbl15 = {}
		local debris = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")
		if not debris then
			return tbl15
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(debris:GetChildren()) do
			local isBasePart = child:IsA("BasePart")

			if isBasePart and child.Name == "FastOverheadTemplate" then
				local animalOverhead = child:FindFirstChild("AnimalOverhead")
				local displayName = animalOverhead and animalOverhead:FindFirstChild("DisplayName")
				local generation = animalOverhead and animalOverhead:FindFirstChild("Generation")

				if displayName and displayName:IsA("TextLabel") and generation and generation:IsA("TextLabel") then
					local str4 = tostring(displayName.Text or "")
					tbl15[str4] = tbl15[str4] or {}
					tbl15[str4][#tbl15[str4] + 1] = { Position = child.Position, Value = fn37(generation.Text), Overhead = animalOverhead }
				end
			end
		end

		return tbl15
	end

	local function fn41(arg, arg2, arg3)
		local v24, v25, v26 = ipairs(arg3[arg] or {})
		local n8 = 14
		local v27 = nil

		for _, v28 in v24, v25, v26 do
			local magnitude = (v28.Position - arg2).Magnitude

			if magnitude < n8 then
				n8 = magnitude
				v27 = v28
			end
		end

		return v27
	end

	local function fn42(arg, arg2)
		local v24 = tbl9[arg.Name]
		local n8 = type(v24) == "table" and tonumber(v24.Generation) or 0
		if n8 <= 0 then
			arg2 = arg2 and arg2.Value
			return arg2 or 0
		end
		local attribute = arg:GetAttribute("Mutation") or arg:GetAttribute("__mutation")
		local mutation = arg2 and arg2.Overhead:FindFirstChild("Mutation")

		if mutation and mutation:IsA("TextLabel") and mutation.Visible and mutation.Text ~= "" then
			attribute = mutation.Text
		end

		attribute = attribute and tbl10[tostring(attribute)]
		local autoKickMinGeneration = 1

		if type(attribute) == "table" then
			autoKickMinGeneration = 1 + (tonumber(attribute.Modifier) or 0)
		end

		local floatEnabled = false

		for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
			local match = child.Name:match("^_Trait%.(.+)$")
			local v25 = match and (tbl11[match] or tbl11[match:gsub("_", " ")])

			if type(v25) == "table" then
				if match == "Sleepy" or v25.Name == "Sleepy" then
					floatEnabled = true
				else
					autoKickMinGeneration += tonumber(v25.MultiplierModifier) or 0
				end
			end
		end

		local v25 = math.round(n8 * autoKickMinGeneration * (floatEnabled and 0.5 or 1))
		arg2 = arg2 and arg2.Value
		if arg2 and arg2 > 0 then
			return arg2
		end
		return v25
	end

	local function fn43()
		local tbl15 = {}
		local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
		if not plots then
			return tbl15, 0
		end
		local v24 = fn40()
		local v25, v26, v27 = ipairs(plots:GetChildren())
		local n8 = 0
				local _vm_GR_1, _vm_M_1

		for _, v28 in v25, v26, v27 do
			local v29, v30, v31 = ipairs(v28:GetChildren())
			local _vm_t_2 = (function(iterator, iteratorState, control) return function() local values = table.pack(iterator(iteratorState, control)); control = values[1]; if control == nil then return false end; return true, table.unpack(values, 1, values.n) end end)(v29, v30, v31)

			for _, v32 in v29, v30, v31 do
				local isModel = v32:IsA("Model")

				if isModel and tbl9[v32.Name] then
					local v33 = fn39(v32)

					if v33 then
						local overhead = fn41(v32.Name, v33.Position, v24)
						local v34 = fn42(v32, overhead)

						if v34 > 0 then
							local v35 = tbl9[v32.Name]
							overhead = overhead and overhead.Overhead
							local mutation = overhead and overhead:FindFirstChild("Mutation")
							local visible = mutation and mutation:IsA("TextLabel") and mutation.Visible

							if visible then
								visible = tostring(mutation.Text or "") ~= ""
							end

							if visible then
								visible = string.lower(tostring(mutation.Text or "")) ~= "normal"
							end

							local text = nil
							local floatEnabled = false
							local textColor3 = nil
							local autoKickEnabled = false
							local fontFace = nil
							local textStrokeColor3 = nil
							local textStrokeTransparency = nil
							local color = nil
							local rotation = nil
							local transparency = nil

							if visible then
								text = mutation.Text
								textColor3 = mutation.TextColor3
								autoKickEnabled = mutation.RichText
								fontFace = mutation.FontFace
								textStrokeColor3 = mutation.TextStrokeColor3
								textStrokeTransparency = mutation.TextStrokeTransparency
								local uiGradient = mutation:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIGradient")
								floatEnabled = true
								color = nil
								rotation = nil
								transparency = nil

								if uiGradient then
									color = uiGradient.Color
									rotation = uiGradient.Rotation
									transparency = uiGradient.Transparency
								end
							end

							local traitImages = {}
							local traits = overhead and overhead:FindFirstChild("Traits")

							if traits then
								for _, child in ipairs(traits:GetChildren()) do
									if child:IsA("ImageLabel") and child.Visible and child.Image ~= "" and #traitImages < 6 then
										traitImages[#traitImages + 1] = child.Image
									end
								end
							end

							local rarity = type(v35) == "table" and (v35.Rarity or v35.Tier or v35.Rank) or nil

							local tbl16 = {
								Model = v32,
								Name = v32.Name,
								Value = v34,
								ValueText = fn38(v34),
								Rarity = rarity and tostring(rarity) or "",
								MutationVisible = floatEnabled,
								MutationText = text and tostring(text) or nil,
								MutationColor = textColor3,
								MutationRichText = autoKickEnabled,
								MutationFontFace = fontFace,
								MutationStrokeColor = textStrokeColor3,
								MutationStrokeTransparency = textStrokeTransparency,
							}

							tbl16.MutationGradientColor = color
							tbl16.MutationGradientRotation = rotation
							tbl16.MutationGradientTransparency = transparency
							tbl16.TraitImages = traitImages
							tbl15[#tbl15 + 1] = tbl16

							if n8 < v34 then
								n8 = v34
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

		end

		return tbl15, n8
	end

	local function fn44(arg, arg2)
		fn36(arg, 7, arg2 or "Secret Zebra")
	end

	local function fn45()
		table.clear(obj2)
		local v24, v25 = fn43()

		for _, v26 in ipairs(v24) do
			obj2[v26.Model] = { FirstSeen = -math.huge, Value = v26.Value }
		end

		n6 = v25
	end

	local function fn46()
		local now = os.clock()
		local v24, v25 = fn43()
		local tbl15 = {}
		local n8 = 0

		for _, v26 in ipairs(v24) do
			local v27 = obj2[v26.Model]
			local isNew = v27 == nil
			local floatEnabled = v27 and now - v27.FirstSeen <= lastBackspaceTime and v26.Value > v27.Value
			v26.IsNew = isNew
			v26.IsLateValue = floatEnabled == true

			if isNew then
				obj2[v26.Model] = { FirstSeen = now, Value = v26.Value }
			elseif v27.Value < v26.Value then
				v27.Value = v26.Value
			end

			if not isNew and not floatEnabled and v26.Value > n8 then
				n8 = v26.Value
			end
		end

		local v26 = nil

		for _, v27 in ipairs(v24) do
			if (v27.IsNew or v27.IsLateValue) and v27.Value >= n5 then
				tbl15[#tbl15 + 1] = v27

				if v27.Value > n8 and (not v26 or v27.Value > v26.Value) then
					v26 = v27
				end
			end
		end

		n6 = v25

		if stealIgnoreOwnBase and v26 then
			fn44(v26)
		elseif not stealIgnoreOwnBase then
			table.sort(tbl15, function(arg, arg2)
				return arg.Value > arg2.Value
			end)

			for _, v27 in ipairs(tbl15) do
				fn44(v27)
			end
		end
	end

	local function fn47()
		stealPriorityEnabled = false

		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end
	end

	local function fn48()
		fn47()
		fn45()
		stealPriorityEnabled = true
		n7 = os.clock() + stealPriorityRadius

		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not stealPriorityEnabled or os.clock() < n7 then
				return
			end
			n7 = os.clock() + stealPriorityRadius
			pcall(fn46)
		end)
	end

	local function fn49()
		fn47()
		local _vm_M_1 = -1

		for i_ = #tbl8, 1, -1 do
			local v24 = tbl8[i_]

			if v24 and v24.Parent then
				v24:Destroy()
			end

			tbl8[i_] = nil
		end

		if v16 and v16.Parent then
			v16:Destroy()
		end

		v16 = nil
		v17 = nil
	end

	v15.Event:Connect(fn49)
	chilliBrainrotNotificationRuntim.Destroying:Connect(fn49)

		Default = false,

			else

		end,
	})
end

		if arg2 ~= nil then
			str4 = tostring(arg2)
		elseif v18 and v18.GetUnit then
			local unit = v18:GetUnit()

				str4 = tostring(unit)

	end

		end
	end

				cleanup:Fire()

		end

			chilliOptimizerRuntime:Destroy()

	local folder = Instance.new("Folder")

	local function fn25(arg, arg2, arg3)

			return
		end

	end

	local function fn26(arg, arg2, arg3)
		local tbl15 = obj3[arg]

		if not tbl15 then
			tbl15 = {}
			obj3[arg] = tbl15
		end

		if tbl15[arg2] == nil then
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return arg[arg2]
			end)

			if not ok then
				return
			end
			tbl15[arg2] = { Value = result }
		end

		pcall(function()
			arg[arg2] = arg3
		end)
	end

	local function fn27(arg)

			return
		end

			return
		end

	local function fn30(arg, arg2)

	end

	local function fn31()

				for k, v22 in v19, v20, v21 do

	end

	local function fn32()

			return
		end

		fn28()

		if thread then
			pcall(task.cancel, thread)

		end

		fn31()
		local _vm_M_1 = -1

	local function fn33()
		if autoKickEnabled then
			return
		end
		autoKickEnabled = true
		autoKickMinGeneration += 1
		local v19 = autoKickMinGeneration
		fn29()

		playerConnections[#playerConnections + 1] = Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if autoKickEnabled and autoKickMinGeneration == v19 then
				task.defer(function()
					if autoKickEnabled and autoKickMinGeneration == v19 then
						fn27(descendant)
					end

		playerConnections[#playerConnections + 1] = Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if autoKickEnabled and autoKickMinGeneration == v19 then

					if autoKickEnabled and autoKickMinGeneration == v19 then

			if not fn30(Workspace, v19) then

			end
			fn30(Lighting, v19)
		end)
	end

do
	local autoKickEnabled = false
	local connection2 = nil
	local connection3 = nil
	local playerConnections = {}
	local characterConnections = {}

	local function fn25(arg)
		return arg:IsA("Accessory") or arg:IsA("Clothing") or arg:IsA("ShirtGraphic")
	end

	local function fn26(arg)
		if autoKickEnabled and arg.Parent and fn25(arg) then
			pcall(function()
				arg:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end

	local function fn27(arg)
		if not autoKickEnabled or not arg then
			return
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
			fn26(descendant)
		end
	end

	local function fn28(player)
		local v19 = playerConnections[player]

		if v19 then
			v19:Disconnect()
			playerConnections[player] = nil
		end

		local v20 = characterConnections[player]

		if v20 then
			v20:Disconnect()
			characterConnections[player] = nil
		end
	end

	local function fn29(arg, arg2)
		local v19 = playerConnections[arg]

		if v19 then
			v19:Disconnect()
		end

		playerConnections[arg] = arg2.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if autoKickEnabled and fn25(descendant) then
				task.defer(fn26, descendant)
			end
		end)

		fn27(arg2)
	end

	local function fn30(player)
		fn28(player)

		characterConnections[player] = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character_)
			if autoKickEnabled then
				fn29(player, character_)
			end
		end)

		if player.Character then
			fn29(player, player.Character)
		end
	end

	local function fn31()
		autoKickEnabled = false

		if connection2 then
			connection2:Disconnect()
			connection2 = nil
		end

		if connection3 then
			connection3:Disconnect()
			connection3 = nil
		end

		for k in pairs(characterConnections) do
			fn28(k)
		end

		for k in pairs(playerConnections) do
			fn28(k)
		end
	end

	local function fn32()
		if autoKickEnabled then
			return
		end
		autoKickEnabled = true
		connection2 = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(fn30)
		connection3 = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(fn28)

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			fn30(player)
		end
	end

	fn2(fn31)

	v18:CreateToggle({
		Name = "Hide Player Cosmetics",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(arg)
			if arg then
				fn32()
			else
				fn31()
			end
		end,
	})
end

do
	local v19 = ServerTab:CreateSection({ Name = "ServerTab", Expanded = true })

	local function fn25()
		if type(queue_on_teleport) == "function" then
			return queue_on_teleport
		end

		if type(queueonteleport) == "function" then
			return queueonteleport
		end

			return syn.queue_on_teleport
		end

		if type(fluxus) == "table" and type(fluxus.queue_on_teleport) == "function" then
			return fluxus.queue_on_teleport
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function fn26(arg)
		pcall(function()
			TeleportService:SetTeleportSetting("__ChilliAutoLoadScriptEnabled", arg)
		end)

		if not arg then
			return true
		end
		local v20 = fn25()
		if not v20 then
			return false
		end

		if rawget(_G, "__ChilliAutoLoadQueued") ~= true then
			if not pcall(v20, [[local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local enabled = true
pcall(function()
    enabled = TeleportService:GetTeleportSetting("__ChilliAutoLoadScriptEnabled") == true
end)
if enabled then
    local ok, source = pcall(function()
        return game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tienkhanh1/spicy/main/Chilli.lua")
    end)
    if ok and type(source) == "string" then
        local chunk = loadstring(source)
        if chunk then
            chunk()
        end
    end
end
]]) then
				return false
			end

			_G.__ChilliAutoLoadQueued = true
		end

		return true
	end

					fn26(false)

						return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(str7))

							playerConnections[#playerConnections + 1] = { Id = str8, Playing = huge }
						end
					end

						break
					end

					if not nextPageCursor or nextPageCursor == "" then
						break
					end
				end

					autoKickEnabled = false

					return
				end

					fn26(true)
				end

						end

					end)

					TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, id, localPlayer)

	})

			return false
		end

		end

end
